      <title>EP236 Accelerated SIEM Journey: A SOC Leader's Playbook for Modernization and AI</title>
      <itunes:title>EP236 Accelerated SIEM Journey: A SOC Leader&#39;s Playbook for Modernization and AI</itunes:title>
      <pubDate>Mon, 28 Jul 2025 14:11:00 +0000</pubDate>
      <guid isPermaLink="false"><![CDATA[07a9c394-7fe3-4df1-a95e-1a3d1cf7ff36]]></guid>
      <link><![CDATA[https://cloudsecuritypodcast.libsyn.com/ep236-accelerated-siem-journey-a-soc-leaders-playbook-for-modernization-and-ai]]></link>
      <description><![CDATA[<p dir="ltr">Guest:</p> <ul> <li dir="ltr" role="presentation"><a href= "https://www.linkedin.com/in/manija-poulatova-165a5966/">Manija Poulatova</a>, Director of Security Engineering and Operations at Lloyd's Banking Group</li> </ul> <p dir="ltr">Topics:</p> <ul> <li dir="ltr" role="presentation"><a href= "https://security.googlecloudcommunity.com/community-blog-42/migrate-off-that-old-siem-already-3902"> SIEM migration</a> is hard, and it can take ages. Yours was - given the scale and the industry - on a relatively short side of 9 months. What's been your experience so far with that and what could have gone faster? </li> <li dir="ltr" role="presentation">Anton might be a "reformed" analyst but I can't resist asking a three legged stool question: of the people/process/technology aspects, which are the hardest for this transformation? What helped the most in solving your big challenges? </li> <li dir="ltr" role="presentation">Was there a process that people wanted to keep but it needed to go for the new tool?</li> <li dir="ltr" role="presentation">One thing we talked about was the plan to adopt <a href= "https://cloud.google.com/chronicle/docs/detection/composite-detections"> composite alerting techniques</a> and what we've been calling the "funnel model" for detection in Google SecOps. Could you share what that means and how your team is adopting? </li> <li dir="ltr" role="presentation">There are a lot of moving parts in a D&R journey from a process and tooling perspective, how did you structure your plan and why?</li> <li dir="ltr" role="presentation">It wouldn't be our show in 2025 if I didn't ask at least one AI question!  What lessons do you have for other security leaders or Fiction?</a></li> <li dir="ltr" role="presentation"><a href= "https://cloud.withgoogle.com/cloudsecurity/podcast/ep125-will-siem-ever-die-siem-lessons-from-the-past-for-the-future/"> EP125 Will dir="ltr" role="presentation"><a href= "https://medium.com/anton-on-security/maverick-scorched-earth-siem-migration-ftw-8a1467cb5501"> "Maverick" — Scorched Earth SIEM Migration FTW!</a> blog</li> <li><a href="https://www.hackthebox.com/">"Hack the box"</a> site</li> </ul>]]></description>
      
      <content:encoded><![CDATA[<p dir="ltr">Guest:</p> <ul> <li dir="ltr" role="presentation"><a href= "https://www.linkedin.com/in/manija-poulatova-165a5966/">Manija Poulatova</a>, Director of Security Engineering and Operations at Lloyd's Banking Group</li> </ul> <p dir="ltr">Topics:</p> <ul> <li dir="ltr" role="presentation"><a href= "https://security.googlecloudcommunity.com/community-blog-42/migrate-off-that-old-siem-already-3902"> SIEM migration</a> is hard, and it can take ages. What's been your experience so far with that and what could have gone faster? </li> <li dir="ltr" role="presentation">Anton might be a "reformed" analyst but I can't resist asking a three legged stool question: of the people/process/technology aspects, which are the hardest for this transformation? What helped the most in solving your big challenges? </li> <li dir="ltr" role="presentation">Was there a process that people wanted to keep but it needed to go for the new tool?</li> <li dir="ltr" role="presentation">One thing we talked about was the plan to adopt <a href= "https://cloud.google.com/chronicle/docs/detection/composite-detections"> composite alerting techniques</a> and what we've been calling the "funnel model" for detection in Google SecOps. Could you share what that means and how your team is adopting? </li> <li dir="ltr" role="presentation">There are a lot of moving parts in a D&R journey from a process and tooling perspective, how did you structure your plan and why?</li> <li dir="ltr" role="presentation">It wouldn't be our show in 2025 if I didn't ask at least one AI question! What lessons do you have for other security leaders preparing their teams for the AI in SOC transition? </li> </ul> <p dir="ltr">Resources:</p> <ul> <li dir="ltr" role="presentation"><a href= "https://cloud.withgoogle.com/cloudsecurity/podcast/ep234-the-siem-paradox-logs-lies-and-failing-to-detect/"> EP234 The SIEM Paradox: Logs, Lies, and Failing to Detect</a></li> <li dir="ltr" role="presentation"><a href= "https://cloud.withgoogle.com/cloudsecurity/podcast/ep197-siem-decoupled-or-not-and-security-data-lakes-a-google-secops-perspective/"> EP197 SIEM (Decoupled or Not), and Security Data Lakes: A Google SecOps Perspective</a></li> <li dir="ltr" role="presentation"><a href= "https://cloud.withgoogle.com/cloudsecurity/podcast/ep231-beyond-the-buzzword-practical-detection-as-code-in-the-enterprise/"> EP231 Beyond the Buzzword: Practical Detection as Code in the Enterprise</a></li> <li dir="ltr" role="presentation"><a href= "https://cloud.withgoogle.com/cloudsecurity/podcast/ep184-one-week-siem-migration-fact-or-fiction/"> EP184 One Week SIEM Migration: Fact or Fiction?</a></li> <li dir="ltr" role="presentation"><a href= "https://cloud.withgoogle.com/cloudsecurity/podcast/ep125-will-siem-ever-die-siem-lessons-from-the-past-for-the-future/"> EP125 Will SIEM Ever Die: SIEM Lessons from the Past for the Future</a></li> <li dir="ltr" role="presentation"><a href= "https://cloud.withgoogle.com/cloudsecurity/podcast/ep223-ai-addressable-not-ai-solvable-reflections-from-rsa-2025/"> EP223 AI Addressable, Not AI Solvable: Reflections from RSA 2025</a></li> <li dir="ltr" role="presentation"><a href= "https://medium.com/anton-on-security/maverick-scorched-earth-siem-migration-ftw-8a1467cb5501"> "Maverick" — Scorched Earth SIEM Migration FTW!</a> blog</li> <li><a href="https://www.hackthebox.com/">"Hack the box"</a> site</li> </ul>]]></content:encoded>
